# Management-Governance Module — TTWO (Synthesis)

## Abstract

Take-Two's leaders are long-tenured and candid about numbers already printed, but they are not aligned stewards of shareholder capital. Five years of capital allocation netted minus $14.6m of free cash flow while the share count rose 61.7%, book value per share fell 42.5%, and $6,665.1m — 84.7% of all goodwill ever recorded — was written off. Pay rewards size, not return on capital; insiders own 0.95%, all granted, and sold $85.2m with zero buys. The biggest signal is structural: $79.5m a year leaves for the CEO's own firm, which supplies him and the President. Audit, contingency and forensic specialists never ran, so those readings are unproven rather than clean. Verdict: misaligned or weak stewardship.

---

## 1. Stewardship Verdict

- **Verdict:** **Misaligned or weak stewardship** — size-based cash and second-equity incentives, an independently rebuilt record of per-share value destruction, insider ownership of 0.95% none of which was bought, and a $79.5m-a-year related-party contract that supplies the two most senior executives.
- **NON-NEGOTIABLE GATE: PASS** (coverage-limited). Computed from the four defined conditions:
  1. **Hard disqualifier from `business-model/01_disqualifier-scan`? NO.** Verbatim: *"No disqualifier triggered."* · *"Any disqualifier triggered: **N**"* · *"**No lock applies.** The synthesizer should carry forward only the Section 4 near-miss and the two routed data gaps named in Section 2."*
  2. **Any Critical red flag fired in this module? NO.** Twelve flags fired, all at **High**. The four person-level flags resting on proven facts (RF-PPL-001/002/004/008) attach **solely to the founder and two former officers whose links to the company terminated in 2007** and who hold no role; MODULE_RULES scopes the gate to *"facts about this company's own controllers and KMP"*, and `07` tested **RF-NET-003 and recorded explicitly that it does not fire**. They are dated base-rate context on the issuer's own enforcement history, not a live finding, and they are recorded here at High with that qualifier attached rather than laundered into or out of the current roster. **RF-MGT-005 fires at High on the live item and is a conviction cap, not a gate failure** (MODULE_RULES: allegations do not fail the gate).
  3. **Any controller / CEO / CFO / chair graded Disqualifying? NO.** Strauss Zelnick (Executive Chairman **and** CEO) grades **Minor concerns on his own record**; the CFO, Corporate Secretary, President and Chief Legal Officer all grade Clean.
  4. **Material undisclosed legal matter (A9-10 / A16-04 Red on the undisclosed prong)? NO — on the evidence reachable.** `12` graded A9-10 **Amber** and **deliberately did not fire RF-CMP-001**, because no matter simultaneously clears materiality, is confirmed on a primary court record, and is absent from the filings; the apparent federal securities class action rests on plaintiff-firm notices, which are not court records, and no primary docket was reachable. That restraint is preserved here, not hardened.
  - **Conditional trigger the master synthesizer must hold open:** confirm a filed securities class action on a court docket and find it still absent from the next periodic filing → A9-10 becomes **Red**, **RF-CMP-001 fires**, Disclosure candor caps at 50, Governance risk floors at 60, and **the gate result must be recomputed**.
- **Hard disqualifier flagged (business-model/01)?** **N** — reported verbatim above. The Governance-risk floor of 80 and the "Serious governance concerns" cap from the deference rule therefore **do not apply on that basis**.

## 1A. Module Disconfirmation *(CLAUDE.md §8)*

- **Strongest bear point:** capital allocation, rebuilt from filings rather than inherited — five years of free cash flow netting to **minus $14.6m** while the share count rose **61.7%**, book value per share fell **42.5%** ($32.90 → $18.91), EBITDA per share fell **26.7%** even as absolute EBITDA rose 17.4%, and **$6,665.1m was written off = 84.7% of all goodwill ever recorded** against the Zynga deal at ≈$9,168m (2.3× the pre-deal capital base, 43.2% dilution, $3,248.9m of new debt, $516.1m of cash interest = 91.5% of four years' operating cash flow) [`02`, 02-001 to 02-010, 02-020; `FY26 10-K, Note 9`; `FY26 10-K, Item 5 — Stock Performance Graph`].
- **Strongest bull point (the steelman, and it is real):** the formal governance machinery is better than the verdict implies and the company is not hiding anything findable. Board **90% independent** with all key committees 100% independent, a Lead Independent Director convening eleven independent-only sessions, **one share one vote, 99.6% float, no promoter, no bloc, no pledge anywhere in the record**, **zero** company-level enforcement in five years, **12/12 10-Qs and 4/4 10-Ks on time with zero NT filings in the issuer's entire history**, say-on-pay at **95%**, **4.66% RPT/say-on-pay dissent**, **zero** loans, guarantees, royalties or promoter-linked customers, and **0 of 54 network subjects classified `disclosable-and-omitted`** [`05`; `07` §4; `09`; `12` 12-001, 12-005].
- **Single killer risk specific to stewardship:** **capture, not leakage** (§24 Filter 6 is tested and *not* tripped — there is no controlling owner). The CEO and President are supplied by contract from the CEO's own firm at **$79.5m a year to 2029-03-31**, priced with **no disclosed benchmarking, no peer set and no performance thresholds**, with **no disinterested-shareholder vote**, policed by a board of **12.6-year average tenure** including an audit-committee chair at 12.7 years, and funded through the one plan shareholders pushed back hardest on (**22.40% against, 2025**) with no disclosed board response to that vote.
- **Disconfirming evidence already visible:** the deal-making that produced the write-down has been **dormant roughly 2.5 years** (business acquisitions $(2.6)m FY2026, **nil** in H1 FY2027) and FY2026's goodwill test passed with no impairment; the **FY2026 operating-cash-flow recovery to +$624.3m** and EBITDA doubling to $788m is one delivered year of inflection; **relative TSR against the NASDAQ-100 over three years, paying zero below the 40th percentile, carries 50.1% of the CEO vehicle's equity at target** — a genuine per-share test with a real floor; and the insider-selling inference is **not proven**: there was **no insider sale between 2026-08-18 and today's launch**, and the two largest CEO sales came *after* the FY26 10-K had already disclosed the November date.

**Upstream output quality:** every one of the nine completed specialists provided a valid Universal Findings Table in the MODULE_RULES schema (`07` using the `TTWO-07-nnn` ID form). No agent failed the schema, so no confidence reduction is applied on that ground. The confidence reduction applied in Section 1 is for the **three specialists that produced no output at all** (`08`, `10`, `11`) and for the pool-integrity defects.

## 2B. The Governance Checklist (assembled — Hard Rule)

Every one of the **179** registry items appears exactly once, in registry order, mechanically matched by ID, carrying the OWNER's verdict. Items whose owner never ran read **"Not run ({agent} missing — run budget exhausted)"** and are **Not proven from available data** — never clean.

## 3. Reconciliation

Five genuine disagreements. In each case the premise is tested, not inherited, and where a cap's factual premise is false it is **released with the evidence named**; where coverage really is thin the cap **stays**.

**1. Incentive alignment — triage cap of 50 vs `03`'s recommendation of 70. RULING: the cap's premise is FALSE; released and reset at 70, which does not bind.**
The triage's trigger is "No proxy / compensation disclosure." That condition is not satisfied. `03` sourced **audited** `FY26 10-K Note 3` (counterparty, term to 2029-03-31, $3.3m annual fee, $13.2m maximum bonus, $16.6m / $10.2m / $6.5m consulting expense) and `Note 16` (unit counts, grant-date values, metric families, measurement windows); the **actual award agreements were filed WITH the 10-K** as `Ex. 10.8 (TSR)` and `Ex. 10.9 (RCS)` with their **complete Annex A vesting grids in the pool** (40/50/75th percentile → 0/50/100/200%; RCS 3/6/9% → 50/100/200%); and `03` read the **real DEF 14A on EDGAR** (`ttwo-20260727.htm`) for say-on-pay 95%, the clawback's reach to ZMC, and double-trigger-only vesting. That is real, audited, citable pay structure. The residual gap is narrower and genuine — **no Summary Compensation Table, no CD&A weights for the named executives, no primary-source pay ratio, no compensation peer group** — so the cap is **reset to 70, sized to that residual**, rather than removed. `03`'s evidence-built **53** sits below 70, so **the cap does not bind and the composite is unchanged either way** (a literal cap of 50 would move the component three points and the Governance Score by 0.33).

**2. RPT & leakage risk — triage floor of 40 + Confidence 80, which `09` refused to apply. RULING: `09`'s refusal was CORRECT.**
The floor's trigger is "No related-party note / RPT disclosure (A5 not quantifiable)." That premise is false on direct evidence: `FY26 10-K Note 3 — MANAGEMENT AGREEMENT` **is** a related-party note in the audited financial statements, quantified **three years deep**, and the real DEF 14A's "Certain Relationships and Related Person Transactions" section names the arrangement and the $275,000-per-month base fee. The triage looked for a note titled "related party" (ASC 850's usual heading); the disclosure sits under its own heading. **A5 IS quantifiable, so the row does not engage.** The practical point `09` makes and I adopt: the derived **44** happens to sit above 40 anyway, but it matters that the number is **derived from evidence, not set by a cap**, because the same false premise also carried a Confidence cap that must not travel. **The Confidence cap of 80 from that row is released too**; the binding Confidence ceiling is `12`'s 75, for a different and real reason.

**3. A2-01…A2-05 and A10-03 recorded "Not Available" by triage. RULING: the gap is CLOSED and the triage record is superseded.**
`05` obtained the real **DEF 14As (2026-07-27 and 2025-07-28)** plus **Item 5.07 voting-results 8-Ks for the 2024, 2025 and 2026 annual meetings** and answered **25 of 25 owned items (14 Green, 6 Amber, 2 Red, 3 genuine N/A)**. Committee composition, independence, financial expertise, meeting counts, chair rotation and three years of resolution-by-resolution vote counts are all now on the record. No cap on the board read survives. One qualifier `05` imposes on itself travels with it: **the decisive evidence is primary but OUT OF POOL** (read on EDGAR 2026-11-19), and three sub-prongs stayed unreadable even with web access — **private auditor sessions, D&O insurance, and the special-meeting / written-consent ownership thresholds**.

**4. Legal-database sweep caps — the triage recorded its own web lookups as unreachable; `07` and `12` both report access WAS available. RULING: the blanket cap is NOT inherited as written; narrower, evidence-sized caps bind instead.**
The triage's "web/legal-database sweep did not run → People & network integrity max 65 / Legal risk floor 40 / Confidence max 70" and its stricter discovery variant ("People max 60 / Confidence max 75") both rest on "all network hosts denied this run." That was true for the triage and false for `07` and `12`, which reached EDGAR, SEC litigation releases, SEC IAPD, USPTO, OpenSanctions, court aggregators, dated media and the company's own site. So the **A17-01 "loop did not run" cap of 60 does not apply on that ground — the loop DID run** (Phase 1 on 6 of 7 recipes, Phase 2 on 3 entities, which is how the barred founder was found at all). What survives is narrower and real:
- **People & network integrity max 60** — but on a *different* and correctly-evidenced trigger: **declared breadth overflow including Tier-A-controlled entities** (26 hop-1 candidates against a 25-entity limit; six named overflows, four of them Tier-A-controlled). `07`'s uncapped sum of 65 is capped to 60. The coverage-limited sweep cap of 65 is also live and less restrictive.
- **Legal & regulatory risk floor 40** — **retained**, because the coverage genuinely is thin where it matters most: **five primary systems were unreachable** (Law.com paywalled, Trellis 403, UniCourt 405, PACER, state/Chancery e-filing) and the axis A9-10 turns on is exactly the one that did not run. `12`'s own evidence rows build to **25**; the reported **40** is the floor. **The 15-point gap is our access, not a finding against the company**, and the master synthesizer must carry it that way.
- **Confidence max 75** — `12`'s own cap, retained as the binding ceiling.
The triage's **A17-01 "Insufficient Data, explicitly NOT Red"** call is upheld and the distinction preserved: source unavailability is a coverage gap, never the Red band.

**5. `05`'s shareholder friendliness of 70 against its own two Red items. RULING: 70 stands, and the tension is stated rather than averaged.**
The honest reading is that **form and substance diverge**. The formal machinery genuinely earns a low-strong score: 90% independent, all key committees 100% independent, a Lead Independent Director convening eleven independent-only sessions, eleven board meetings, a skills matrix with no gap, annual elections with majority voting and a resignation policy, no pill, no classified board, one share one vote, no supermajority, special-meeting and written-consent rights retained, every resolution across three meetings passed, no director below 90.1% support, and the one pay item that drew 13.9% dissent in 2024 engaged on and fixed to 95.3% by 2026. The two Reds are both about **the same thing**: the board policing the ZMC agreement is not an arm's-length counterparty to the man who has chaired it for eighteen years — combined Chair/CEO (A1-02) and four long-tenured independents including the audit chair (A1-04). `05`'s own verdict word is **"adequate," not "strong,"** and that is what the 70 means: the lower end of the 61–80 band. It is not averaged away — it is carried into the Governance Score at an 0.11 weight against a Capital allocation of 31 at 0.14, which is why the composite lands at 52 / Weak rather than anywhere near the shareholder-friendliness score itself.

**One non-disagreement worth recording, because it could easily be mistaken for one.** `01` did **not** emit RF-MGT-005 (nothing was routed from `business-model/01`, which states "none found, hard or soft … nothing soft to route") while `07` **did** emit it at High on a different item it discovered itself (the Zelnick civil claim). Both are correct on their own facts and they do not conflict: `01` tested the *routed* trigger and `07` tested the *substance* of §24 Filter 1. The flag fires, the cap applies, and `01`'s record stands unamended.

**Most-restrictive resolution where caps overlap:** People & network integrity faced ceilings of 70, 65, 65 and 60 → **60 binds**. Confidence faced 80, 80, 80, 75 and 70-not-inherited → **75 binds as the ceiling, set at 65 on the evidence**. Audit & assurance faced a cap of 65 and a substitution of 50 → **50 binds**. Capital allocation faced a ceiling of 50 against an evidence score of 31 → **31 stands, cap does not bind**. Disclosure candor faced 70, 65 and 60 → **60 binds**.

**Forensic tag propagation (CLAUDE.md §13; eval check AQ).** Every tag `06` emitted is propagated here as a standalone line, so the master synthesizer's cross-module forensic roll-up can detect a compounding accounting-integrity mosaic that no single module's cap would catch:

RF-DISC-001 (commentary contradicting the numbers) — FIRED, High

RF-DISC-002 (recurring "one-off" / aggressive non-GAAP add-backs) — FIRED, High

RF-REG-002 (delayed results / material-disclosure timeliness) — **TESTED AND NOT FIRED.** `12` owns A7-01 and graded it **Green** on primary EDGAR dates: 12/12 on-time 10-Qs, 4/4 on-time 10-Ks, **zero NT filings in the issuer's entire history**, proxy filed 2026-07-27 against a stated 2026-07-29 deadline, no exchange nudge. `06` adds nothing contradicting it. **The candor finding does not contaminate the timeliness finding.**

**Cross-module forensic context the master synthesizer should fold in alongside the above:** `earnings/06_earnings-quality` scores earnings quality **48/100**, and `balance-sheet-survival/05_off-balance-sheet-and-contingencies` fired **RF-OBS-001 (contingent-liability spike)**. Those are other modules' flags, named here because `11` and `10` never ran in this one and the mosaic is exactly what their absence would otherwise hide.

## 5. Stewardship Summary

**(a) Promises and per-share value.** They deliver on the one number they choose to guide and nowhere else: **six-for-six on their own final annual Net Bookings guidance FY2021–FY2026**, against GAAP diluted EPS kept in **1 of 4** years (missed by $16.06 and $21.08) and operating cash flow in **3 of 6** (worst a −99.7% miss, $350m guided and $1.1m delivered). Capital allocation then destroyed per-share value on arithmetic that is not close: **minus $14.6m of five-year free cash flow, share count +61.7%, book value per share −42.5%, EBITDA per share −26.7% while absolute EBITDA rose 17.4%, and $6,665.1m written off = 84.7% of all goodwill ever recorded** — with the company's own five-year scorecard putting $100 at **$111.77 against $169.11 for the NASDAQ Composite**. The best use of capital was debt reduction, and even that carries a qualifier: **the FY2026 $1,150.0m repayment was funded by selling equity at $225.00, not by operating cash.**

**(b) Do incentives and ownership point at per-share value or at size?** At size, with one real exception. The cash bonus (to **$13.2m**) pays on **absolute Adjusted EBITDA**; the second equity metric pays on **Recurrent Consumer Spending, a revenue slice, on whichever of two grids pays more**; a third of the units at target are time-based with no metric; and **nothing anywhere pays on return on capital, earnings per share or cash flow per share while return on capital ran −4.3% / −3.1% / −4.2% / −0.8% across FY2023–FY2026 and −1.0% on the latest twelve months.** The exception is genuine and should not be buried: **relative total shareholder return against the NASDAQ-100 over three years, paying zero below the 40th percentile, carries 50.1% of the CEO vehicle's equity at target.** Ownership points nowhere useful: **0.95% aggregate, CEO plus ZMC 0.72%, every share granted, none bought, $85.2m sold across thirteen open-market sales with zero purchases.**

**(c) Are minority holders protected, and is management candid when results are bad?** Protected in form, exposed in substance. One share one vote, 99.6% float, no promoter, no bloc, **no pledge anywhere in the record**, no poison pill, annual elections with majority voting, every resolution passed across three meetings, and **0 of 54 network subjects classified `disclosable-and-omitted`**. Against that: a **$79.5m-a-year contract supplying the two most senior executives, priced with no disclosed benchmarking, peer set or performance thresholds, with no disinterested-shareholder vote**, policed by a board of 12.6-year average tenure including an audit-committee chair at 12.7 years, and funded through the one plan shareholders pushed back hardest on (**22.40% against, 2025**, with no disclosed response to that vote). On candor the split is sharp and it is the tell: in its worst reported quarter management led with the line it missed and quantified the variance, and disclosed both GTA VI slips against their own superseded dates — yet the **Q1 FY2027 release called a quarter with Net Bookings −3%, recurrent spending −1% and a GAAP loss 186.6% wider "excellent… positive trends"**, and the **legal note of three consecutive filings names zero cases** while the public record holds a **$44.9m adverse IBM verdict against Zynga**, a **five-week UK blacklisting trial with judgment pending today**, and **at least one apparent federal securities class action** that appears in no filing.

**(d) The single most important reason not to trust this team with shareholder capital — and the honest counterweight.** The reason: **they have spent the last five years converting shareholders' ownership into scale that did not earn a return, and the pay structure does not ask them to stop.** $9,856.7m of acquisition consideration and $2,476.7m of capitalised development, funded by $5,196.7m of new debt and $1,449.3m of new equity rather than by the business, against four consecutive years of negative return on capital and a bonus that pays on absolute EBITDA. The counterweight, stated because it is real: the deal-making has been **dormant roughly 2.5 years**, FY2026's goodwill test passed, FY2026 operating cash flow recovered to **+$624.3m**, there has been **no insider sale since 2026-08-17** including through today's launch, and **nothing the engine could reach suggests these particular people are dishonest** — nineteen years of unqualified audit opinions, zero company-level enforcement in five years, and a clean sweep on every person-level axis that was reachable. The live integrity item is **one unresolved, un-adjudicated private civil claim against the chief executive in his personal investment capacity, whose pleadings nobody in this run could read**. That is a monitoring-and-cap finding, not a walk-away — and not a clean bill of health either.

**Gate consistency check (required).** The gate reads **PASS** and the Critical count is **0** — these agree, so there is no self-contradiction to reconcile. The four person-level flags resting on proven facts are recorded at **High** and not Critical for one stated reason: MODULE_RULES scopes gate condition 2's Critical severity to facts touching **this company's own controllers and KMP** (the same scope condition 3 uses), and all three subjects' links **terminated in 2007** with none holding any role. `07` tested **RF-NET-003 on both bands and recorded explicitly that it does not fire**. Grading a 19-year-dead, fully-adjudicated, fully-remediated episode as a live Critical would manufacture a gate failure out of base-rate history — the mirror image of the error the engine bans in the other direction.

DMA Design*, a £1.5m **breach-of-contract** action in the Scottish Court of Session (~1997), decided against **DMA Design**. **Classification: `predecessor_entity` — a DIFFERENT legal entity, a solvent corporate seller divesting a unit on 1998-03-12** | **coverage-limited** (no UK/US registry, no primary docket) | **None.** No Disqualifying- or Material-equivalent fact exists, so neither RF-NET-003 band fires and neither banded cap binds. **Not counted a second time as direct company enforcement under A9-01/A9-02** | **PROVISIONAL** — still-pending; **does NOT close `resolved-clean`** because Coverage is not `full` |

No enforcement, no insolvency. **Classification: IN-GROUP live subsidiary**, named in `FY26 10-K, Ex. 21.1` — so its record is part of the consolidated group's record, **not an arm's-length lineage exposure** | **coverage-limited** (UK Companies House and Scottish court primary records unreachable) | **None.** A 29-year-old pre-acquisition contract judgment clears no materiality band | **PROVISIONAL** — still-pending |

**Classification check, stated so it cannot be mistaken (the critical one).** `12` establishes that **there is NO `former_name_of_listco` subject in this network**: EDGAR's `formerNames` field for CIK 0000946581 returns `[]`, independently re-confirmed on 2026-11-20, and the company has filed as "TAKE TWO INTERACTIVE SOFTWARE INC" throughout. So **no name carries the direct-scoring treatment**, and nothing from a predecessor's record is counted a second time as this company's own enforcement under A9-01/A9-02 or allowed to fire a Critical company-level gate. **No row closes `resolved-clean`, because no row reads Coverage `full`** — a partial sweep that found nothing has established "not found on the axes that ran," not "clean." The MODULE_RULES `pending-12-reconciliation` row therefore applies in its **partial** form: `12` DID run and DID report per-predecessor, so the "nobody ran it" condition is not met, but the grades stay **PROVISIONAL** and the coverage cap is live (see Section 4).

**The riskiest person, and whether any grade trips a gate or cap.** **Strauss Zelnick** — Executive Chairman, CEO, and principal of the firm the company pays $79.5m a year. His grade is **Minor concerns on his own record**, for an **unresolved, un-adjudicated** private civil fraud claim whose pleadings nobody in this run could read. **No grade trips the Non-Negotiable Gate** (no current controller, CEO, CFO or chair is Disqualifying, and no current officer is Material concerns); the grade fires **RF-MGT-005 at High**, which caps Disclosure candor at 60 and caps conviction at "no rating above Watchlist."

**How far the check actually reached, and what it did not cover.** It reached EDGAR, SEC litigation releases, SEC IAPD, USPTO, OpenSanctions, court-record aggregators, dated media and the company's own website, and it found the barred founder and two barred former officers that nothing in the evidence pool disclosed. It did **not** reach: every state corporate registry (Delaware, New York), every **primary court docket** (Law.com paywalled, Trellis 403, UniCourt 405, PACER and the Chancery e-filing system unreachable), the OFAC tool directly, every professional-licence register, the **D-5 registered-address cluster recipe** (nothing asserted either way), ZMC Advisors' **Form ADV Schedule A**, and six named hop-1 entities. **That is a coverage-limited read, and the §24 Filter 1 signal is open** — which is why conviction is capped rather than the finding being dressed up as either exoneration or indictment.

## 5D. Analyst Follow-Up Questions

**On the ZMC management agreement (A5-05 Amber, A5-10 Amber, A1-02 Red, A6-01 Red, RF-SHR-001):** Was any benchmarking or peer analysis performed before the $79.5m price was approved, and will it be disclosed? Is the arrangement recurring or does it end at 2029-03-31, and what replaces it? Is it material to earnings and cash — yes: 1.19% of revenue, 9.09% of total G&A, **20.6% of all stock compensation**. Is the disclosure adequate — quantified in Note 3, but the approval policy, benchmarking and performance thresholds are **not readable**. Is it company-specific or sector-wide — **company-specific**; no peer in the sector supplies its CEO by contract from the CEO's own private-equity firm. Does it affect minority holders — yes, through the equity plan they voted **22.40% against**. Is management's explanation credible — there is no explanation to assess, only an assertion of arm's length, and **an assertion is not evidence**.

**On the legal note (A9-10 Amber, A9-11 Amber, 06-012 Red):** **One paid primary-docket pull is the single highest-value next request** — the federal securities class actions over the GTA VI delay disclosures, and the Glasgow Employment Tribunal register for *IWGB v. Rockstar Games UK*. If a securities class action is on file, its total absence from a 10-Q filed **2026-11-09** moves A9-10 from Amber to **Red**, fires **RF-CMP-001**, caps Disclosure candor at 50, floors Governance risk at 60, **and forces the gate to be recomputed**. Separately: why does the MD&A name "the IBM case against Zynga" twice while the legal note never names the **$44.9m** verdict? What is the tribunal judgment, and will an independent review be commissioned?

**On capital allocation (02's eight Red rows, RF-CAP-004, RF-CAP-002, RF-FIN-004):** One-off or recurring — the write-down is one episode, but the **dilution is recurring at ~1.5% a year with zero offset**. Is there a plan to use the 10.0m shares still authorised? Will return on capital clear zero in FY2027, and on what pre-order volume — **unquantified anywhere in the pool**? Will the residual $1,061.9m of goodwill and $1,631.2m of acquired intangibles survive the next test?

**On incentives (A6-01 Red, A6-04 Amber, A6-05 Amber, 03-010 Red):** Will any returns-based or per-share metric (ROIC, EPS, FCF per share) enter the plan? What are the annual bonus thresholds, which the 10-K describes only as "certain performance thresholds"? What is the CEO pay ratio from the primary source, and what is the compensation peer group?

**On ownership and insider behaviour (04-005 Red, 04-006 Red, 04-008 Insufficient Data):** Obtain the **8-K / material-event feed** so the second GTA VI delay can be dated and the 2026-03-02 sale cleared or not — today it is **NOT PROVEN, neither clean nor damning**. Were any of the thirteen sales under a Rule 10b5-1 plan (the Form 4 covers would settle it)? Does any insider buy after the launch?

**On the three absent specialists:** Run `08`, `10` and `11`. The specific questions their absence leaves open: the **E&Y audit vs non-audit fee split on a 30-year tenure** (A4-06/07), whether there has been any **restatement** (A7-02), the **movement, crystallization and provided-versus-only-disclosed split** of contingencies (A7a-13/14, which the balance-sheet-survival substitute does **not** cover), and the **Beneish M-score and Dechow F-score battery**, which was never computed — **RF-ACC-001 is untested, not cleared**.

**On the group structure (A11-01 Amber, A11-02 Amber, A5-08 Amber):** **Name the counterparty** to the $62.1m non-cash purchase of non-voting shares that quintupled the unconsolidated investment balance in six months. Publish an ownership chain for the 145 entities. What is the business purpose of the ~20 subsidiaries that disclose none, and what is the repatriation cost of the 82.2% of cash held offshore while all $2,500m of notes sit at the parent?

**On the board (A1-04 Red, A1-07 Amber, A1-13 Amber, A2-01 Amber):** What is the refreshment plan for the audit-committee chair at 12.7 years and the three directors seated since March 2007? Why only **four** audit-committee meetings? Disclose per-director attendance, D&O cover, and the special-meeting ownership threshold. What is the CEO succession process?

## 6. What Would Change The Stewardship Verdict?

| Current Verdict | What Would Strengthen It | What Would Weaken It | Data Needed |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Misaligned or weak stewardship** | (a) A returns-based metric (ROIC, EPS or FCF per share) entering the incentive plan with real weight; (b) **buybacks resuming at scale** so the 61.7% dilution starts reversing — 10.0m shares are already authorised; (c) **return on capital clearing zero** and staying there for two to three years after the launch, with free cash flow decisively positive; (d) the 2029 ZMC renewal put to a **disinterested-shareholder vote** with disclosed benchmarking and performance thresholds, or the executives brought onto the payroll; (e) the roles of Chair and CEO **separated**; (f) the audit-committee chair and the three 2007-vintage directors refreshed; (g) **insiders buying in the open market** with their own cash rather than selling grants; (h) the legal note **naming its material matters**; (i) a clean docket pull resolving 24STCV20137 in the CEO's favour, which would lift RF-MGT-005 and its candor cap; (j) `08`, `10` and `11` running clean on primary evidence | (a) A **confirmed** federal securities class action over the GTA VI delay disclosures still absent from the next periodic filing → **RF-CMP-001 fires, candor caps at 50, Governance risk floors at 60, the gate is recomputed**; (b) **a new material acquisition**, especially debt-funded, which would move RF-CAP-004 from dormant to live; (c) an adverse UK tribunal judgment with an uncapped blacklisting award and no independent review; (d) the GTA VI cycle failing to convert into positive return on capital, confirming that scale was bought and never earned; (e) the docket pull revealing conduct touching Zelnick's **fiduciary** role rather than his private investments → re-grade from Minor to Material or worse, which engages the Material-concerns caps; (f) **any goodwill impairment on the residual $1,061.9m**; (g) `11` running and the Beneish/Dechow battery coming back in the manipulator zone → ForensicsRisk floor 70; (h) resumption of insider selling into a disappointing launch | **1.** The complaint, FAC and full docket in **LA Superior Court 24STCV20137** plus the pro se complaint in **Delaware Chancery 2025-0816** (one paid pull). **2.** A primary-docket search for the **federal securities class actions**, plus the **Glasgow Employment Tribunal register**. **3.** The **TTWO 8-K / material-event feed** (the pool holds Alphabet's by mistake) — it dates the second GTA VI delay and closes the insider-timing test. **4.** The **full DEF 14A body** — Summary Compensation Table, CD&A metric weights, CEO pay ratio, compensation peer group, director-fee table. **5.** Dispatch **`08`, `10` and `11`.** **6.** The **E&Y audit vs non-audit fee split.** **7.** A governance peer set from `business-model/08`. **8.** **Quantified GTA VI pre-order and launch-week volumes.** **9.** An ownership chain for the 145 group entities and the name of the $62.1m counterparty |

## 7. Note To The Final Synthesizer

- **The stewardship verdict is "Misaligned or weak stewardship," and the single strongest piece of evidence is arithmetic, not narrative:** five years of free cash flow netting to **minus $14.6m** while the share count rose **61.7%** and **$6,665.1m — 84.7% of all goodwill ever recorded — was written off**, with the company's own five-year table putting $100 at **$111.77 against $169.11 for the NASDAQ Composite**. `02` rebuilt this from the filings rather than inheriting `business-model/11`'s quick-read.
- **Non-Negotiable Gate: PASS, coverage-limited.** No hard disqualifier (`business-model/01` verbatim: *"No disqualifier triggered." · "Any disqualifier triggered: N" · "No lock applies."*); **0 Critical red flags**; **0 current controllers/CEOs/CFOs/chairs graded Disqualifying**; no confirmed material undisclosed legal matter. **One conditional trigger stays live:** confirm a filed securities class action on a court docket and find it still absent from the next periodic filing → RF-CMP-001 fires, candor caps at 50, Governance risk floors at 60, **and the gate must be recomputed**. Do not treat the PASS as settled while that docket is unpulled.
- **Capital allocation destroyed per-share value and the pay structure did not ask it to stop.** BVPS −42.5% ($32.90 → $18.91), EBITDA per share −26.7% while absolute EBITDA rose 17.4%, revenue +89.9% against revenue per share +18.5%, four consecutive years of negative return on capital. Two qualifiers travel with it: the M&A pattern has been **dormant ~2.5 years** and FY2026's goodwill test passed; and the "best use of capital," the $1,150.0m debt repayment, was **funded by selling equity at $225.00, not by operating cash**.
- **Incentives and ownership do NOT align management with minority holders — with one genuine exception.** Cash bonus on **absolute Adjusted EBITDA** (to $13.2m); second equity metric on **Recurrent Consumer Spending**, on whichever of two grids pays more; **nothing on return on capital, EPS or cash flow per share**. The exception: **relative TSR vs the NASDAQ-100 over three years, zero below the 40th percentile, carries 50.1% of the CEO vehicle's equity at target** — better than most. Ownership: **0.95% aggregate, 0.72% for CEO + ZMC (~$291m), every share granted, none bought, $85.2m sold with zero buys.** **The Summary Compensation Table reports the CEO at $403,019 while his firm received $66.8m (proxy basis) / cost $79.5m (audited basis) — anyone reading the standard table understates his economics by roughly two orders of magnitude.**
- **The people read.** 23 subjects: **19 Clean, 1 Minor concerns, 0 Material concerns, 3 Disqualifying**. The riskiest live subject is **Strauss Zelnick**, graded **Minor concerns on his own record** for an **unresolved, un-adjudicated** civil fraud / California-securities claim (LA Sup. Ct. 24STCV20137, stayed to arbitration 2025-02-04) plus a pro se Delaware derivative action. **The three Disqualifying grades belong to the founder and two former officers, all permanently SEC-barred, with every link terminated in 2007 — dated base-rate context on the issuer's own enforcement history, NOT a live finding; RF-NET-003 was tested and explicitly does not fire.** §24 Filter 1 is **open**: RF-MGT-005 fires at High, capping Disclosure candor at 60 and conviction at "no rating above Watchlist." It is a conviction cap, **not a gate failure**.
- **The checklist read.** **114/179 answered (63.7%) — 64 Green · 43 Amber · 7 Red · 65 not available.** The seven Reds: **A1-02** (combined Executive Chairman/CEO), **A1-04** (four IDs past ten years including the audit-committee chair at 12.7), **A6-01** (pay rising 34.1% through four loss years), and **A16-03 / A16-04 / A16-06 / A16-18** — all four of those **historical only, links terminated 2007**. **The binding Checklist Risk is RPT & leakage risk at 44**: max(44, 40 substituted, 40 substituted, 40) = **44**, so the two substituted 40s do not move the composite — but they were entered, not dropped, because unknown is not safe.
- **The biggest governance risk is capture, not leakage, and it is High severity.** **$79.5m a year leaves for the controller's own firm — $16.6m cash consulting expense ($3.3m base fee at $275,000/month plus a bonus at/near its $13.2m cap) plus $62.9m of non-cash stock compensation on RSUs granted to ZMC** — which is 20.6% of all stock comp for two of 12,909 people, 1.19% of revenue, $205.2m over three years, 100% controller-linked. It is priced with **no disclosed benchmarking, peer set or performance thresholds**, carries **no disinterested-shareholder vote**, is policed by a board of **12.6-year average tenure**, and is funded through the one plan shareholders voted **22.40% against** with no disclosed response to that vote. **Carry the build with the total every time, and never treat the $16.6m, $66.8m and $79.5m figures as interchangeable.**
- **Hard disqualifier from `business-model/01_disqualifier-scan`: verbatim — "No disqualifier triggered." · "Any disqualifier triggered: N" · "No lock applies. The synthesizer should carry forward only the Section 4 near-miss and the two routed data gaps named in Section 2."** Not re-decided here.
- **§24 rejector filters: two tripped, four did not.** **Filter 4 (serial acquirer) TRIPPED** — RF-CAP-004 confirmed on rebuilt evidence; CapAlloc ceiling 50 (31 sits below, does not bind), **GovRisk floor 60 BINDS**. **Filter 1 (integrity) TRIPPED** — RF-MGT-005 at High; **Disclosure candor capped 63 → 60**, Management quality ceiling 60 (55 below), conviction cap "no rating above Watchlist." **Filter 2 (turnaround) NOT tripped** — no new management, no new plan; classified **product-cycle inflection under incumbent management**, and if you reclassify it a turnaround the cap binds with no change to the score (55 < 60). **Filter 6 (unaligned owner) NOT tripped** — no controlling owner at all. **Filter 3 (debt/survival) belongs to balance-sheet-survival**, but note this module's own finding: **TBVPS −$6.55 against a peer median of +$3.20, and 8 of 10 peers in net cash while TTWO carries net debt** — the opposite of Filter 3's preference. **Filter 5 (fast-changing industry) is the business-model module's.**
- **Three specialists FAILED / were ABSENT: `08_audit-and-assurance-quality`, `10_contingent-liabilities-and-commitments`, `11_accounting-forensics`. They were not dispatched because the run's budget was exhausted after the other nine. This is an execution gap — NOT a reasoned verdict on the evidence and NOT a finding about the company.** 53 registry items are therefore **Not proven from available data**, never clean: **A4 (all 15), A7-02, A7a (all 15), A8 (all 20), A14-01, A14-02.** The composite kept the nine-component formula with three named substitutions (**Audit & assurance quality 50**; **Contingent-liability risk 40** and **Accounting-forensics risk 40** into the Checklist Risk max) and **Confidence capped at 80** by that rule. **`11`'s absence means the Beneish M-score and Dechow F-score battery was never computed, so RF-ACC-001 is UNTESTED, NOT CLEARED. `10`'s absence means off-P&L exposure was not sized at the governance layer**; `balance-sheet-survival/05_off-balance-sheet-and-contingencies.md` partially covers the exposure sizing ($714.8m recognized, $1,567.8m maximum = 44.7% of equity, $740.1m of commitments, $274.5m gross UTB across eight open years, **RF-OBS-001 fired there**) and is used **only as a labelled substitute from another module** — it carries no provisioning-candor, movement or disclosure-honesty read, so the A7a bands are not applied to it.
- **Partial-data caps applied, and what each limits:** *RF-MGT-005 (§24 Filter 1)* — **the one cap that moves a number**, Disclosure candor 63 → 60, plus the conviction cap. *RF-CAP-004* — GovRisk floor 60 binds. *Declared breadth overflow including Tier-A-controlled entities* — People & network integrity capped 65 → 60 (six named overflows: Third Way, Naylor LLC, K Property Group LLC, Second City Capital Partners, Vail Resorts, MGM Resorts). *Legal-database sweep coverage-limited* — **Legal & regulatory risk floored at 40 over evidence rows building to 25; the 15-point gap is our access, not a finding against the company.* Confidence 75 ceiling.* *`pending-12-reconciliation` partial form* — all three lineage grades stay **PROVISIONAL**; **no row closes `resolved-clean` because no row reads Coverage `full`**. *Thin transcript base* — only **two** transcripts, both inside FY2027, and zero shareholder letters, so the candor cap binds **confidence**, not the number. **Three caps were RELEASED on falsified premises, each justified by name:** the no-proxy incentive cap of 50 (audited Note 3 + Note 16, Ex. 10.8/10.9 Annex A grids, real DEF 14A on EDGAR → reset to 70, which does not bind); the no-RPT-note floor of 40 and its Confidence cap of 80 (Note 3 quantifies three years); and the no-board-disclosure cap (`05` answered 25 of 25 owned items from the real DEF 14As and three Item 5.07 8-Ks). **`00`'s triage recorded caps on premises three later specialists disproved — do not re-inherit them from the triage.**
- **Pool-integrity facts that must travel into any data-sufficiency read:** the pool's **DEF 14A export is an empty vendor file**; the pool's **"Key Developments" export belongs to Alphabet Inc. (GOOGL)**, so there is **no TTWO material-event / 8-K history in the pool at all**; only **two transcripts** exist, both inside FY2027; and **this extract generation carries NO `numeric_reading_check` on any source or sheet, with no reading annotations in `corpus.txt` — every reading is UNCHECKED, never verified clean.** No single amount is currently not-proven on that ground, but the uncertainty is unresolved and travels forward. Several specialists legitimately sourced primary documents from **EDGAR that are absent from the pool** (the real DEF 14As, three Item 5.07 8-Ks, the 2022-05-03 8-K, SEC litigation releases) — those are **primary-but-out-of-pool** and labelled as such throughout.
- **Biggest missing data point — ONE item: the complaint, first amended complaint and full docket in *Marks Capital, Inc. & Steven Marks v. Harry Strauss Zelnick* (LA Superior Court 24STCV20137), plus the pro se complaint in *Marks v. Zelnick* (Delaware Chancery 2025-0816).** One paid docket pull. It is the only document set that can move the Executive Chairman & CEO's grade in either direction, and the same pull answers the securities-class-action question that is the only live path to a gate recomputation. Everything else outstanding — the full DEF 14A body, the 8-K feed, the E&Y fee split, the three absent specialists — is cheaper, important, and cannot change a grade.
- **EXPLICIT HANDOFF: this module supersedes the `business-model/11_capital-allocation-governance` quick-read where they overlap. Treat THIS module's governance verdict and scores as the primary governance read.** `02` rebuilt the serial-acquirer test from the filings rather than inheriting `business-model/11`'s score of 75 and **confirms it on its own evidence**; `business-model/11`'s $79.5m ZMC figure is corroborated and reconciled here to its components. Carry forward: **Governance Score 52 / Confidence-Adjusted 34 / Governance Rating Weak / Gate PASS (coverage-limited) / 12 red flags, 0 Critical / checklist coverage 63.7%.**

## 8. Simple Summary

- **Promises: half kept.** They hit their own sales target six years out of six — and missed their own profit target in three of the last four years and their own cash target in three of six, once by 99.7%.
- **Capital destroyed value per share.** Over five years the business threw off **minus $14.6m of spare cash** while the number of shares grew **61.7%**. Book value per share fell **42.5%**. Of every dollar of goodwill the company ever recorded, **84.7% has been written off**.
- **Pay rewards getting bigger, not earning a return.** The cash bonus (up to **$13.2m**) pays on total profit before interest, tax and write-offs — a size measure. **Nothing in the pay plan pays on the return earned on the money invested**, which has been negative four years running. The one good part: half the chief executive's shares only pay if the stock beats the NASDAQ-100 over three years.
- **Insiders own almost none of it and have been selling.** Together they own **0.95%** of the company. Every share was given to them; essentially none was bought. In twelve months they sold **$85.2m** across thirteen sales and bought **nothing**. The two most senior executives are supplied to the company by a private firm the chief executive owns and runs, which was paid **$79.5m** last year — while the standard pay table shows his pay as **$403,019**.
- **Minority shareholders are protected on paper, exposed in practice.** One share one vote, no controlling owner, no shares pledged, nine of ten directors independent, every shareholder vote carried. But the **$79.5m contract has no published price comparison and shareholders never vote on it**, and the board approving it has sat together for **12.6 years on average**.
- **Candor is split.** They own a miss once the number is printed — and the written release called a quarter with sales down 3% and a loss 186.6% wider "excellent." **The legal section of three filings in a row names no case at all**, while the public record holds a **$44.9m court loss**, a five-week UK employment trial with judgment due, and an apparent shareholder lawsuit that appears nowhere in the filings.
- **No hard disqualifier was flagged.** `business-model/01_disqualifier-scan`: *"No disqualifier triggered… No lock applies."* The company's founder was convicted and permanently barred — **but that was 2007 and every link ended then**; nobody running the company today has an adverse record on anything we could check.
- **Useful, with three holes you must know about.** **The audit specialist, the contingent-liabilities specialist and the accounting-forensics specialist never ran — the run's budget ran out.** Fifty-three checklist items are **unanswered, not clean**, including the audit-fee test and the whole fraud-detection battery. **Those tests were not run; they were not passed.**

# red_flags.csv
red_flag_id,trigger,severity,evidence,source_and_date,score_impact,follow_up
"RF-CAP-004","Serial-acquirer / value-destructive M&A pattern, debt-funded near or above own value (CLAUDE.md s24 Filter 4)","High","Zynga ~$9,168m = ~2.3x total capitalization of $4,059.9m at 2022-03-31; $3,248.9m of new debt; 43.2% dilution; $6,665.1m written off = 84.7% of all goodwill ever recorded; $516.1m cash interest = 91.5% of four years' CFO; Private Division sold at an $11.4m loss; studios shut, two titles cancelled. CONFIRMED on independently rebuilt filing evidence, not inherited. Pattern DORMANT ~2.5 years and FY2026's goodwill test passed","FY25 10-K Stockholders' Equity and Note 20; FY26 10-K Note 9 and Item 1A; CIQ Historical Capitalization — 2026-11-20","Capital allocation ceiling 50 (evidence score 31 sits below, so the cap does not bind); Governance risk FLOOR 60 BINDS","Is any new deal contemplated post-launch, and does the residual $1,061.9m of goodwill survive FY2027?"
"RF-FIN-004","Goodwill impairment","High","$6,665.1m of acquisition-related write-downs, $5,887.3m of it goodwill attributed by the filing to 'one of our reporting units' WITHOUT NAMING IT; GAAP EPS missed by $16.06 (FY2024) and $21.08 (FY2025) behind $2,342.1m and $3,545.2m of charges","FY26 10-K Note 9; FY25 10-K; CIQ Guidance tab — 2026-11-20","Capital allocation 31; Management quality 55","Does the FY2027 goodwill test hold?"
"RF-CAP-002","Buybacks not reducing share count","High","ZERO repurchases in FY2023-FY2026 and H1 FY2027 with 10.0m shares of a 21.7m authorisation still available, against a share count +61.7% and ~8.7m shares of comp-related issuance wholly unoffset","FY26 10-K Item 5; CIQ Historical Capitalization — 2026-11-20","Capital allocation 31","Will any authorisation be used after the launch cash arrives?"
"RF-CAP-001","Large acquisition with vague rationale","High (pool-limited)","No quantified synergy target readable in this pool for a ~$9,168m deal. 02 graded the row Insufficient Data (pool-limited), so the trigger rests on the ABSENCE of a readable rationale, not on evidence of a vague one","FY26 10-K Item 1A; FY25 10-K Note 20 — 2026-11-20","Capital allocation 31","Was a synergy target ever published?"
"RF-SHR-001","High votes against a key resolution","High (owner 05 graded severity Medium; escalated here to the registry's conservative High/Critical band per CLAUDE.md s4, with 05's reasoning preserved: 'the 20% line was crossed once, on one resolution, in a three-meeting record where everything else cleared 86%')","22.40% AGAINST the 2025 Stock Incentive Plan amendment (77.6% for) — THE VERY PLAN THROUGH WHICH THE ZMC RESTRICTED STOCK UNITS ARE GRANTED. The board's disclosed response is a year-round engagement programme (56.4% of shares contacted, 31.8% in director-led discussions) that demonstrably lifted say-on-pay 86.2% -> 95.1% -> 95.3%, but it DOES NOT ADDRESS THE EQUITY-PLAN OUTCOME ANYWHERE. Also 16.37% against the 2026 officer-exculpation amendment","Item 5.07 8-Ks ttwo-20250918.htm and ttwo-20260917.htm — read on EDGAR 2026-11-19, absent from the pool","Shareholder friendliness 70 (A10-03 Amber)","Will the board respond specifically to the plan vote, and will the 2029 ZMC renewal go to a majority-of-minority vote?"
"RF-DISC-001","Management commentary contradicts the numbers","High","The Q1 FY2027 release called a quarter with Net Bookings -3%, recurrent consumer spending -1% and a GAAP loss 186.6% WIDER 'excellent... positive trends'; the Q2 release headline and CEO quote omit the guidance miss the call owned two hours later","TTWO 1Q27 Earnings Release, 2026-08-06, CEO Commentary; TTWO 2Q27 Earnings Release, 2026-11-09","Disclosure candor 60","Does the written channel repeat it on the post-launch quarter?"
"RF-DISC-002","Recurring 'one-off' / aggressive non-GAAP add-backs","High","Four add-back lines recur in EVERY period, including acquisition expense 4.5 years post-close and the same software-development impairment line four years running, with a $189.4m reversal swing flattering FY2026; $90.0m/quarter of stock compensation excluded = 32.5% of the headline measure, guided to $462m (29.5%) for FY2027, against a share count rising 177.1m -> 187.2m. Credit where due: the full line-by-line build of both adjusted layers AND of the forward outlook is published every quarter","TTWO 2Q27 Earnings Release, 2026-11-09, p.2; earnings/06_earnings-quality s4, s5, s8 (earnings quality 48/100)","Disclosure candor 60","Will acquisition expense ever stop being added back?"
"RF-MGT-005","Unresolved adverse integrity signal, not cleared and not proven (CLAUDE.md s24 Filter 1)","High","UNRESOLVED AND UN-ADJUDICATED: Marks Capital, Inc. & Steven Marks v. Harry Strauss Zelnick — civil FRAUD and California Corporate Securities Law claims against the Executive Chairman & CEO PERSONALLY, LA Superior Court 24STCV20137, filed 2024-08-09, STAYED PENDING ARBITRATION 2025-02-04 (so no public merits outcome will ever appear), sanctions motion denied 2026-01-27; plus a PRO SE Delaware Chancery derivative action, Marks v. Zelnick 2025-0816, filed 2025-07-15. NOTHING IS ADJUDICATED; the primary dockets were not reached; the dispute concerns his private investment activity.
